\subsection{1. Purpose of This Document}\label{purpose-of-this-document}

Per the STAT 663 Studio Threshold Requirements for
\textbf{Reproducibility} and \textbf{Integrity} (``Preserve the first
output of anything an assistant produced for you, with the prompt and
date. Do not reprompt to obtain a more auditable failure''), this
document records:

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\tightlist
\item
  \textbf{The Course AI Contribution Log} for the construction and
  statistical refinement of our Virginia Traffic Safety Executive R
  Shiny Dashboard.
\item
  \textbf{The Exact Initial Prompt and Preserved First Output
  (\texttt{app.R} v1.0)} generated by Gemini Pro on November 10, 2026.
\item
  \textbf{The Chronological Trail of Human Verification Checks, Defect
  Audits, and Revisions (Iterations 1--6)} showing what our human visual
  and statistical checks established, what failed in the AI's initial
  outputs, how we corrected each failure, and what residual concerns
  remain.
\item
  \textbf{The Final Verified Dashboard Code (\texttt{app.R} v6.0)}
  shipped after human audit.
\end{enumerate}

\begin{center}\rule{0.5\linewidth}{0.5pt}\end{center}

\subsection{2. Course AI Contribution
Log}\label{course-ai-contribution-log}

{\def\LTcaptype{none} % do not increment counter

\end{minipage} & \begin{minipage}[b]{\linewidth}\raggedright
Tool/model \& date
\end{minipage} & \begin{minipage}[b]{\linewidth}\raggedright
AI contribution
\end{minipage} & \begin{minipage}[b]{\linewidth}\raggedright

\midrule\noalign{}
\endhead
\bottomrule\noalign{}
\endlastfoot
\textbf{1. Combine M1/M2 prototype R scripts and Word outline into a
3-page executive R Shiny dashboard (First Output)} & Gemini Pro,
11/10/2026 & Generated initial 3-page \texttt{bslib} R Shiny dashboard
(\texttt{app.R}) combining Page 1 county/weather summaries and
\texttt{CIPI}/\texttt{CIPIrate}/\texttt{SARWC} plots, Page 2 county
time-series and weather plots, and Page 3 Leaflet street map; fixed
0-accident months in conditional rates (\texttt{NA} instead of
\texttt{0}) and matched counties by 5-digit Census \texttt{GEOID}. &
Audited the rendered app in RStudio and identified four major visual,
geospatial, and statistical defects requiring human revision across
subsequent iterations (crowded error bars on bar charts, blank Leaflet
map on rural counties, exploding \texttt{0–100\%} CIs on Page 2 weather,
and meaningless PIs). & Ran initial \texttt{app.R} against
\texttt{Virginia\_Accidents\_With\_Pop.csv}; verified timestamp parsing,
\texttt{GEOID} joins, and reactivity across county tiers. & Initial
output mixed point-estimate bars with error bars and failed on sparse
county-weather-month combinations. \\
\textbf{2. Separate Confidence Intervals from top-row bar charts and
adjust for multiple comparisons} & Gemini Pro, 11/10/2026 & Explained
statistical and perceptual reasons why layering black Clopper-Pearson
binomial CIs onto solid \texttt{ggplotly} bars crowded Page 1; removed
\texttt{geom\_errorbar()} from top-row bar charts on Pages 1 and 2,
isolated intervals in dedicated panels, added Page 2 CI/PI panel, and
added Bonferroni and Šidák FWER adjustments. & Directed the design rule
that all CIs and PIs must be isolated in dedicated uncertainty charts
rather than layered onto magnitude bar charts, and required family-wise
error rate (\(\text{FWER} = 5\%\)) adjustments across simultaneous
comparisons. & Visually verified in Shiny that top-row bar charts
rendered cleanly without overlapping whiskers and checked that
individual CI levels scaled dynamically as \(1 - (0.05 / m)\). &
Bonferroni adjustment is conservative when monthly weather or county
series are correlated. \\
\textbf{3. Troubleshoot and fix Page 3 Leaflet map rendering blank on
individual counties} & Gemini Pro, 11/10/2026 & Identified that
\texttt{validate(need(nrow(streets\_df)\ \textgreater{}\ 0))} wiped the
entire map blank when rural counties had \(<5\) accidents per street,
that filtering out the other 132 counties removed statewide context, and
that \texttt{CartoDB.Positron} tiles were washed out; updated code to
always draw all 133 VA counties, use OpenStreetMap/Esri tiles, lock
camera zoom via \texttt{fitBounds()}, and auto-fallback to
\texttt{min\ =\ 1} street accident. & Tested individual counties on Page
3, caught that the map went blank and lost statewide Virginia context,
and requested a real road map with statewide visibility and county
click-to-zoom functionality. & Clicked through urban and rural Virginia
counties in the live app to confirm the crimson county boundary,
surrounding VA counties, street markers, and OpenStreetMap road network
rendered without blanking out. & Street-level hotspot coordinates use
the median GPS coordinate of accidents sharing a street name, which
summarizes long interstates (e.g., I-95, I-81) as a single county
centroid point. \\
\textbf{4. Diagnose and fix \texttt{0\%} to \texttt{100\%} exploding CIs
and PIs for rare weather conditions on Page 2} & Gemini Pro, 11/10/2026
& Confirmed user's degrees-of-freedom diagnosis (rare weather strings
like \texttt{"Heavy\ T-Storm\ /\ Windy"} occurred in only \(N = 2\) or
\(3\) active months in a county, giving \(\text{df} = 1\) or \(2\) and
Bonferroni \(t\)-critical values of \(12.6\) to \(101.8\), while
1-accident months created binary \(0\%/100\%\) variance); proposed
consolidating weather regimes and weighting monthly log-odds. & Audited
Page 2 interval widths, identified the \texttt{0\%–100\%} anomaly on
\texttt{"Heavy\ T-Storm\ /\ Windy"}, and diagnosed that rare weather
conditions lacked sufficient active monthly observations (\(\text{df}\))
to support a monthly \(t\)-interval. & Inspected \texttt{N\_Units}
(active months) and CI bounds in the Page 2 \texttt{DT} table across
multiple counties to verify that \(\text{df}\) starvation was the root
cause. & First logit transformation introduced a continuity-correction
centering shift (\texttt{+\ 0.5}) that required further human audit in
Task 5. \\
\textbf{5. Audit one-sided CI bug and simplify Page 2 monthly weather CI
methodology for executive interpretability} & Gemini Pro, 11/10/2026 &
Identified that adding \texttt{+\ 0.5} to monthly log-odds pulled
small-count interval centers toward \(50\%\) away from the pooled mean
dot; replaced complex logit math with three plain-English rules using
the textbook \(\text{Mean} \pm t \cdot \text{SE}\) formula: (1) 5 Core
Weather Categories, (2) \(\ge 5\) accidents for a month to qualify, and
(3) \(\ge 10\) qualifying months (\(\text{df} \ge 9\)) per bar. & Caught
visually that several CIs appeared one-sided around the point estimate;
rejected black-box transformations and directed a simpler, transparent
specification that an average policymaker can understand while
guaranteeing sufficient \(n\) per bar. & Verified that all red
point-estimate dots sat strictly at the center of their symmetric
\(t\)-intervals and that every plotted weather category had \(n \ge 10\)
qualifying months in major counties. & In very small rural counties with
fewer total crashes, fallback thresholds (\(\ge 2\) accidents/month,
\(\ge 3\) months) are triggered so the chart does not render empty. \\
\textbf{6. Remove uninformative Prediction Intervals (PIs) and fix
Plotly error-bar whisker collisions} & Gemini Pro, 11/10/2026 & Removed
all PI calculations, gray error bars, table columns, and labels from
Pages 1 and 2; changed \texttt{geom\_errorbar(width\ =\ 0.2)} to
\texttt{width\ =\ 0} so \texttt{ggplotly} renders clean horizontal CI
bars without oversized vertical end-caps merging across adjacent
categories. & Evaluated PIs across Pages 1 and 2 and determined they
were effectively meaningless for the policy decision; ordered removal of
PIs from all plots, tables, and labels, and caught the visual bug where
\texttt{width\ =\ 0.2} end-caps merged across rows on Page 2. & Verified
in the final rendered app and \texttt{DT} tables that only 95\% CIs are
displayed, no PI columns or labels remain, and horizontal CI bars no
longer overlap vertically. & Observational Kaggle streaming data still
reflects reporting and traffic sensor coverage biases that statistical
confidence intervals alone cannot eliminate. \\
\textbf{7. Generate comprehensive statistical \& visual guide for Pages
1 and 2 (Markdown \& PDF)} & Gemini Pro, 11/10/2026 & Produced a
complete inventory of all distinct visuals and tables on Pages 1 and 2
detailing navigation paths, plain-English summaries, statistical
formulas, decision utility for Question 1, and zero-prior-knowledge
step-by-step derivations of non-introductory statistics. & Requested a
structured breakdown distinguishing distinct statistical visuals,
explicitly requiring confirmation of where visuals do not support the
decision (e.g., raw counts in Visual 1A and constant-denominator county
weather ratios in Visual 5B) and step-by-step explanations assuming no
prior knowledge. & Cross-checked every formula and explanation against
the final \texttt{app.R} server logic to ensure 100\% alignment between
the code and documentation. & Static PDF documentation must be updated
if slider defaults are modified prior to the December 1 freeze. \\
\end{longtable}
}

\begin{center}\rule{0.5\linewidth}{0.5pt}\end{center}

\subsection{3. Preserved First AI Output (Unaltered Baseline
Artifact)}\label{preserved-first-ai-output-unaltered-baseline-artifact}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \textbf{Tool / Model:} Gemini Pro
\item
  \textbf{Date:} November 10, 2026
\item
  \textbf{Exact Initial Prompt Submitted:}
\end{itemize}

\begin{quote}
``Attached is a notebook with project information, plus my groups
completion of M1 and M2 project tasks. Also included in this notebook is
a small subset of the full data source in a comma delimited format and R
code we put in a markdown file that represents some of the charts we are
interested in. Finally, attached is a word document that contains our
outline of the structure of a proposed dashboard we'd like to make in R
code using the ideas we coded in the notebook as a basis for the
representative charts we think could be included in the dashboard. Your
job is to combine both into an effective R dashboard. Format the R
dashboard with a modern design and color so that it might be useful in
the situation of giving the dashboard to the governor or other political
resources. Do not assume all the R code in the notebook is correctly
specified but make any corrections needed so that all stats and charts
are correctly specified. Make sure to ask me directly if you have any
questions you need answered about what to put into the dashboard. Output
in R code.''
\end{quote}

\subsubsection{\texorpdfstring{First Output Code (\texttt{app.R} ---
Version 1.0, Preserved
Verbatim)}{First Output Code (app.R --- Version 1.0, Preserved Verbatim)}}\label{first-output-code-app.r-version-1.0-preserved-verbatim}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# ==============================================================================}
\CommentTok{\# VIRGINIA TRAFFIC SAFETY EXECUTIVE DASHBOARD}
\CommentTok{\# Office of the Governor | Highway Safety \& Infrastructure Analytics}
\CommentTok{\# Authors: Matthew Goldenberg \& Maria Wood (STAT 663)}
\CommentTok{\# ==============================================================================}
\CommentTok{\# Required packages:}
\CommentTok{\# install.packages(c("shiny", "bslib", "tidyverse", "lubridate", "plotly",}
\CommentTok{\#                    "leaflet", "sf", "tigris", "DT", "scales", "bsicons"))}

\FunctionTok{library}\NormalTok{(shiny)}
\FunctionTok{library}\NormalTok{(bslib)}
\FunctionTok{library}\NormalTok{(tidyverse)}
\FunctionTok{library}\NormalTok{(lubridate)}
\FunctionTok{library}\NormalTok{(plotly)}
\FunctionTok{library}\NormalTok{(leaflet)}
\FunctionTok{library}\NormalTok{(sf)}
\FunctionTok{library}\NormalTok{(tigris)}
\FunctionTok{library}\NormalTok{(DT)}
\FunctionTok{library}\NormalTok{(scales)}
\FunctionTok{library}\NormalTok{(bsicons)}

\FunctionTok{options}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{tigris\_use\_cache =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{)}

\CommentTok{\# ==============================================================================}
\CommentTok{\# 1. REPRODUCIBLE DATA LOADING \& PREPROCESSING}
\CommentTok{\# ==============================================================================}
\CommentTok{\# Avoid hardcoded setwd() paths so the project runs from a clean checkout (M2/M4)}
\NormalTok{data\_paths }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}
  \StringTok{"../Data/Virginia\_Accidents\_With\_Pop.csv"}\NormalTok{,}
  \StringTok{"Data/Virginia\_Accidents\_With\_Pop.csv"}\NormalTok{,}
  \StringTok{"Virginia\_Accidents\_With\_Pop.csv"}
\NormalTok{)}

\NormalTok{existing\_path }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ data\_paths[}\FunctionTok{file.exists}\NormalTok{(data\_paths)][}\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{]}

\ControlFlowTok{if}\NormalTok{ (}\FunctionTok{is.na}\NormalTok{(existing\_path)) \{}
  \FunctionTok{stop}\NormalTok{(}
    \StringTok{"Data file \textquotesingle{}Virginia\_Accidents\_With\_Pop.csv\textquotesingle{} not found. "}\NormalTok{,}
    \StringTok{"Ensure it is located in \textquotesingle{}../Data/\textquotesingle{} or \textquotesingle{}Data/\textquotesingle{} relative to this script."}
\NormalTok{  )}
\NormalTok{\}}

\CommentTok{\# Read data; treat empty strings as NA so blank weather/street cells are caught}
\NormalTok{acc\_raw }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{read.csv}\NormalTok{(existing\_path, }\AttributeTok{na.strings =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{""}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"NA"}\NormalTok{), }\AttributeTok{stringsAsFactors =} \ConstantTok{FALSE}\NormalTok{)}

\CommentTok{\# Clean and standardize variables}
\NormalTok{acc }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ acc\_raw }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}
    \CommentTok{\# Robust timestamp parsing (handles both ISO8601 \textquotesingle{}T/Z\textquotesingle{} and standard formats)}
    \AttributeTok{Start\_Time =} \FunctionTok{ymd\_hms}\NormalTok{(Start\_Time, }\AttributeTok{tz =} \StringTok{"UTC"}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{quiet =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{),}
    \AttributeTok{End\_Time   =} \FunctionTok{ymd\_hms}\NormalTok{(End\_Time, }\AttributeTok{tz =} \StringTok{"UTC"}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{quiet =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{),}
    \AttributeTok{Year       =} \FunctionTok{year}\NormalTok{(Start\_Time),}
    \AttributeTok{month\_year =} \FunctionTok{floor\_date}\NormalTok{(Start\_Time, }\AttributeTok{unit =} \StringTok{"month"}\NormalTok{),}
    
    \CommentTok{\# Severity classification (Severity 3 \& 4 = Severe)}
    \AttributeTok{severe     =}\NormalTok{ Severity }\SpecialCharTok{\textgreater{}=} \DecValTok{3}\NormalTok{,}
    
    \CommentTok{\# Standardize GEOID as 5{-}character string for exact tigris spatial joins}
    \AttributeTok{GEOID      =} \FunctionTok{str\_pad}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{as.character}\NormalTok{(GEOID), }\AttributeTok{width =} \DecValTok{5}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{side =} \StringTok{"left"}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{pad =} \StringTok{"0"}\NormalTok{),}
    
    \CommentTok{\# Clean text fields}
    \AttributeTok{Weather\_Condition =} \FunctionTok{str\_squish}\NormalTok{(Weather\_Condition),}
    \AttributeTok{Street            =} \FunctionTok{str\_squish}\NormalTok{(Street),}
    \AttributeTok{census\_name       =} \FunctionTok{str\_squish}\NormalTok{(census\_name)}
\NormalTok{  ) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{filter}\NormalTok{(}\SpecialCharTok{!}\FunctionTok{is.na}\NormalTok{(census\_name), }\SpecialCharTok{!}\FunctionTok{is.na}\NormalTok{(Severity), }\SpecialCharTok{!}\FunctionTok{is.na}\NormalTok{(Year))}

\CommentTok{\# Pre{-}compute static county population rankings across all VA localities in data}
\NormalTok{county\_pop\_ranks }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ acc }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{group\_by}\NormalTok{(GEOID, census\_name) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{summarise}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{population =} \FunctionTok{first}\NormalTok{( }\FunctionTok{na.omit}\NormalTok{(population) ), }\AttributeTok{.groups =} \StringTok{"drop"}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{arrange}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{desc}\NormalTok{(population)) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{pop\_rank =} \FunctionTok{row\_number}\NormalTok{())}

\NormalTok{n\_localities }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{nrow}\NormalTok{(county\_pop\_ranks)}
\NormalTok{mid\_start    }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{max}\NormalTok{(}\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{, }\FunctionTok{floor}\NormalTok{((n\_localities }\SpecialCharTok{{-}} \DecValTok{5}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{/} \DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{+} \DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{mid\_indices  }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ mid\_start}\SpecialCharTok{:}\FunctionTok{min}\NormalTok{(n\_localities, mid\_start }\SpecialCharTok{+} \DecValTok{4}\NormalTok{)}

\NormalTok{top5\_pop\_names    }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ county\_pop\_ranks }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%} \FunctionTok{slice\_head}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{n =} \DecValTok{5}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%} \FunctionTok{pull}\NormalTok{(census\_name)}
\NormalTok{middle5\_pop\_names }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ county\_pop\_ranks }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%} \FunctionTok{slice}\NormalTok{(mid\_indices) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%} \FunctionTok{pull}\NormalTok{(census\_name)}
\NormalTok{bottom5\_pop\_names }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ county\_pop\_ranks }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%} \FunctionTok{slice\_tail}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{n =} \DecValTok{5}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%} \FunctionTok{pull}\NormalTok{(census\_name)}

\NormalTok{all\_years    }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{sort}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{unique}\NormalTok{(acc}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{Year))}
\NormalTok{all\_counties }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ county\_pop\_ranks}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{census\_name}

\CommentTok{\# Load Virginia county/independent city boundaries once via tigris}
\NormalTok{va\_boundaries }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{tryCatch}\NormalTok{(\{}
  \FunctionTok{counties}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{state =} \StringTok{"VA"}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{cb =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{year =} \DecValTok{2022}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{progress\_bar =} \ConstantTok{FALSE}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{st\_transform}\NormalTok{(}\DecValTok{4326}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{\}, }\AttributeTok{error =} \ControlFlowTok{function}\NormalTok{(e) \{}
  \ConstantTok{NULL}
\NormalTok{\})}

\CommentTok{\# Exact Clopper{-}Pearson 95\% Binomial Confidence Interval helper (vectorized)}
\NormalTok{calc\_binom\_ci }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \ControlFlowTok{function}\NormalTok{(x, n, }\AttributeTok{bound =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"lower"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"upper"}\NormalTok{)) \{}
\NormalTok{  bound }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{match.arg}\NormalTok{(bound)}
\NormalTok{  idx }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{ifelse}\NormalTok{(bound }\SpecialCharTok{==} \StringTok{"lower"}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{)}
  \FunctionTok{map2\_dbl}\NormalTok{(x, n, }\ControlFlowTok{function}\NormalTok{(k, total) \{}
    \ControlFlowTok{if}\NormalTok{ (}\FunctionTok{is.na}\NormalTok{(k) }\SpecialCharTok{||} \FunctionTok{is.na}\NormalTok{(total) }\SpecialCharTok{||}\NormalTok{ total }\SpecialCharTok{\textless{}=} \DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{) }\FunctionTok{return}\NormalTok{(}\ConstantTok{NA\_real\_}\NormalTok{)}
    \FunctionTok{binom.test}\NormalTok{(k, total)}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{conf.int[idx]}
\NormalTok{  \})}
\NormalTok{\}}

\CommentTok{\# Exact Garwood 95\% Poisson Confidence Interval helper for monthly counts}
\NormalTok{calc\_poisson\_ci }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \ControlFlowTok{function}\NormalTok{(k\_vec, }\AttributeTok{bound =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"lower"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"upper"}\NormalTok{)) \{}
\NormalTok{  bound }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{match.arg}\NormalTok{(bound)}
\NormalTok{  idx }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{ifelse}\NormalTok{(bound }\SpecialCharTok{==} \StringTok{"lower"}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{)}
  \FunctionTok{map\_dbl}\NormalTok{(k\_vec, }\ControlFlowTok{function}\NormalTok{(k) \{}
    \ControlFlowTok{if}\NormalTok{ (}\FunctionTok{is.na}\NormalTok{(k) }\SpecialCharTok{||}\NormalTok{ k }\SpecialCharTok{\textless{}} \DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{) }\FunctionTok{return}\NormalTok{(}\ConstantTok{NA\_real\_}\NormalTok{)}
    \FunctionTok{poisson.test}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{as.integer}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{round}\NormalTok{(k)))}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{conf.int[idx]}
\NormalTok{  \})}
\NormalTok{\}}

\CommentTok{\# Colorblind{-}safe executive palette (Okabe{-}Ito \& Commonwealth Navy/Gold/Crimson)}
\NormalTok{exec\_navy    }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \StringTok{"\#0F2042"}
\NormalTok{exec\_crimson }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \StringTok{"\#9E1B32"}
\NormalTok{exec\_teal    }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \StringTok{"\#0072B2"}

\NormalTok{exec\_slate   }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \StringTok{"\#4A5568"}

\CommentTok{\# ==============================================================================}
\CommentTok{\# 2. USER INTERFACE (bslib Modern Executive Layout)}
\CommentTok{\# ==============================================================================}
\NormalTok{ui }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{page\_navbar}\NormalTok{(}
  \AttributeTok{title =}\NormalTok{ tags}\SpecialCharTok{$}\FunctionTok{span}\NormalTok{(}
    \FunctionTok{bs\_icon}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"shield{-}shaded"}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{size =} \StringTok{"1.3rem"}\NormalTok{),}
    \StringTok{" Commonwealth of Virginia | Traffic Severity Analytics"}
\NormalTok{  ),}
  \AttributeTok{theme =} \FunctionTok{bs\_theme}\NormalTok{(}
    \AttributeTok{version =} \DecValTok{5}\NormalTok{,}
    \AttributeTok{primary =}\NormalTok{ exec\_navy,}
    \AttributeTok{secondary =}\NormalTok{ exec\_slate,}
    \AttributeTok{danger =}\NormalTok{ exec\_crimson,}
    \AttributeTok{base\_font =} \FunctionTok{font\_google}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"Inter"}\NormalTok{),}
    \AttributeTok{heading\_font =} \FunctionTok{font\_google}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"Merriweather"}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{  ),}
  \AttributeTok{fillable =} \ConstantTok{FALSE}\NormalTok{,}

  \CommentTok{\# ============================================================================}
  \CommentTok{\# PAGE 1: STATEWIDE \& TIERED COUNTY EXECUTIVE SUMMARY}
  \CommentTok{\# ============================================================================}
  \FunctionTok{nav\_panel}\NormalTok{(}
    \AttributeTok{title =} \StringTok{"1. Statewide \& Population Tier Summary"}\NormalTok{,}
    \AttributeTok{icon =} \FunctionTok{bs\_icon}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"bar{-}chart{-}line{-}fill"}\NormalTok{),}
    \FunctionTok{layout\_sidebar}\NormalTok{(}
      \AttributeTok{sidebar =} \FunctionTok{sidebar}\NormalTok{(}
        \AttributeTok{title =} \StringTok{"Executive Filters"}\NormalTok{,}
        \AttributeTok{width =} \DecValTok{310}\NormalTok{,}
        \FunctionTok{selectInput}\NormalTok{(}
          \StringTok{"p1\_pop\_tier"}\NormalTok{,}
          \StringTok{"1. Locality Population Tier:"}\NormalTok{,}
          \AttributeTok{choices =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}
            \StringTok{"Top 5 in Population"}    \OtherTok{=} \StringTok{"top5"}\NormalTok{,}
            \StringTok{"Middle 5 in Population"} \OtherTok{=} \StringTok{"mid5"}\NormalTok{,}
            \StringTok{"Bottom 5 in Population"} \OtherTok{=} \StringTok{"bot5"}\NormalTok{,}
            \StringTok{"Top 10 in Population"}   \OtherTok{=} \StringTok{"top10"}\NormalTok{,}
            \StringTok{"All Virginia Localities"} \OtherTok{=} \StringTok{"all"}
\NormalTok{          ),}
          \AttributeTok{selected =} \StringTok{"top5"}
\NormalTok{        ),}
        \FunctionTok{selectInput}\NormalTok{(}
          \StringTok{"p1\_year"}\NormalTok{,}
          \StringTok{"2. Analysis Year:"}\NormalTok{,}
          \AttributeTok{choices =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"All Years (2016–2023)"} \OtherTok{=} \StringTok{"All"}\NormalTok{, }\FunctionTok{as.character}\NormalTok{(all\_years)),}
          \AttributeTok{selected =} \StringTok{"All"}
\NormalTok{        ),}
        \FunctionTok{radioButtons}\NormalTok{(}
          \StringTok{"p1\_metric"}\NormalTok{,}
          \StringTok{"3. Primary Display Metric:"}\NormalTok{,}
          \AttributeTok{choices =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}
            \StringTok{"Total Accidents (Raw Count)"}                \OtherTok{=} \StringTok{"total\_accidents"}\NormalTok{,}
            \StringTok{"Severe Accident Rate (\% of Accidents)"}      \OtherTok{=} \StringTok{"severe\_rate"}\NormalTok{,}
            \StringTok{"Severe Accident Ratio (per 100k Residents)"} \OtherTok{=} \StringTok{"severe\_ratio"}
\NormalTok{          ),}
          \AttributeTok{selected =} \StringTok{"severe\_ratio"}
\NormalTok{        ),}
        \FunctionTok{hr}\NormalTok{(),}
        \FunctionTok{selectInput}\NormalTok{(}
          \StringTok{"p1\_ci\_type"}\NormalTok{,}
          \StringTok{"4. Uncertainty / Interval View:"}\NormalTok{,}
          \AttributeTok{choices =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}
            \StringTok{"Monthly Severe Ratio 95\% CI \& PI (CIPI)"}      \OtherTok{=} \StringTok{"CIPI"}\NormalTok{,}
            \StringTok{"Monthly Severe Rate 95\% CI \& PI (CIPIrate)"}   \OtherTok{=} \StringTok{"CIPIrate"}\NormalTok{,}
            \StringTok{"Weather Severe Rate 95\% Binomial CI (SARWC)"}  \OtherTok{=} \StringTok{"SARWC"}
\NormalTok{          ),}
          \AttributeTok{selected =} \StringTok{"CIPI"}
\NormalTok{        ),}
        \FunctionTok{sliderInput}\NormalTok{(}
          \StringTok{"p1\_min\_weather\_n"}\NormalTok{,}
          \StringTok{"Min. Accidents per Weather Condition:"}\NormalTok{,}
          \AttributeTok{min =} \DecValTok{5}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{max =} \DecValTok{100}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{value =} \DecValTok{20}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{step =} \DecValTok{5}
\NormalTok{        ),}
        \FunctionTok{hr}\NormalTok{(),}
\NormalTok{        tags}\SpecialCharTok{$}\FunctionTok{small}\NormalTok{(}
          \AttributeTok{class =} \StringTok{"text{-}muted"}\NormalTok{,}
          \FunctionTok{bs\_icon}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"info{-}circle"}\NormalTok{),}
          \StringTok{" Severe accidents are defined as Severity levels 3 and 4 (major traffic delay impact). "}\NormalTok{,}
          \StringTok{"Population denominators use 2023 U.S. Census Bureau county/city estimates."}
\NormalTok{        )}
\NormalTok{      ),}

      \CommentTok{\# KPI Value Boxes}
      \FunctionTok{layout\_column\_wrap}\NormalTok{(}
        \AttributeTok{width =} \DecValTok{1}\SpecialCharTok{/}\DecValTok{3}\NormalTok{,}
        \FunctionTok{value\_box}\NormalTok{(}
          \AttributeTok{title =} \StringTok{"Total Recorded Accidents (Selection)"}\NormalTok{,}
          \AttributeTok{value =} \FunctionTok{textOutput}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"p1\_kpi\_total"}\NormalTok{),}
          \AttributeTok{showcase =} \FunctionTok{bs\_icon}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"car{-}front{-}fill"}\NormalTok{),}
          \AttributeTok{theme =} \StringTok{"primary"}
\NormalTok{        ),}
        \FunctionTok{value\_box}\NormalTok{(}
          \AttributeTok{title =} \StringTok{"Overall Severe Accident Rate"}\NormalTok{,}
          \AttributeTok{value =} \FunctionTok{textOutput}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"p1\_kpi\_rate"}\NormalTok{),}
          \AttributeTok{showcase =} \FunctionTok{bs\_icon}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"exclamation{-}triangle{-}fill"}\NormalTok{),}
          \AttributeTok{theme =} \StringTok{"danger"}
\NormalTok{        ),}
        \FunctionTok{value\_box}\NormalTok{(}
          \AttributeTok{title =} \StringTok{"Severe Accidents per 100k Residents"}\NormalTok{,}
          \AttributeTok{value =} \FunctionTok{textOutput}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"p1\_kpi\_ratio"}\NormalTok{),}
          \AttributeTok{showcase =} \FunctionTok{bs\_icon}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"people{-}fill"}\NormalTok{),}
          \AttributeTok{theme =} \StringTok{"secondary"}
\NormalTok{        )}
\NormalTok{      ),}

      \CommentTok{\# Row 1 Charts: County Comparison + Weather Severity}
      \FunctionTok{layout\_column\_wrap}\NormalTok{(}
        \AttributeTok{width =} \DecValTok{1}\SpecialCharTok{/}\DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{,}
        \FunctionTok{card}\NormalTok{(}
          \AttributeTok{full\_screen =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{,}
          \FunctionTok{card\_header}\NormalTok{(}
            \AttributeTok{class =} \StringTok{"bg{-}primary text{-}white"}\NormalTok{,}
            \StringTok{"County Comparison by Selected Metric \& Population Tier"}
\NormalTok{          ),}
          \FunctionTok{card\_body}\NormalTok{(}
            \FunctionTok{plotlyOutput}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"p1\_county\_plot"}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{height =} \StringTok{"400px"}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{          ),}
          \FunctionTok{card\_footer}\NormalTok{(}
            \AttributeTok{class =} \StringTok{"text{-}muted small"}\NormalTok{,}
            \StringTok{"Bars show point estimates; error bars on rates show exact 95\% Clopper{-}Pearson binomial confidence intervals."}
\NormalTok{          )}
\NormalTok{        ),}
        \FunctionTok{card}\NormalTok{(}
          \AttributeTok{full\_screen =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{,}
          \FunctionTok{card\_header}\NormalTok{(}
            \AttributeTok{class =} \StringTok{"bg{-}primary text{-}white"}\NormalTok{,}
            \StringTok{"Weather Conditions Most Associated with Severe Accidents"}
\NormalTok{          ),}
          \FunctionTok{card\_body}\NormalTok{(}
            \FunctionTok{plotlyOutput}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"p1\_weather\_plot"}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{height =} \StringTok{"400px"}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{          ),}
          \FunctionTok{card\_footer}\NormalTok{(}
            \AttributeTok{class =} \StringTok{"text{-}muted small"}\NormalTok{,}
            \StringTok{"Filtered dynamically by the selected Population Tier and Year above. Error bars indicate 95\% binomial CIs."}
\NormalTok{          )}
\NormalTok{        )}
\NormalTok{      ),}

      \CommentTok{\# Row 2: Grouped Confidence \& Prediction Intervals (CIPI / CIPIrate / SARWC)}
      \FunctionTok{card}\NormalTok{(}
        \AttributeTok{full\_screen =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{,}
        \FunctionTok{card\_header}\NormalTok{(}
          \AttributeTok{class =} \StringTok{"bg{-}dark text{-}white"}\NormalTok{,}
          \StringTok{"Uncertainty \& Volatility Analysis: Confidence and Prediction Intervals"}
\NormalTok{        ),}
        \FunctionTok{card\_body}\NormalTok{(}
          \FunctionTok{layout\_column\_wrap}\NormalTok{(}
            \AttributeTok{width =} \DecValTok{1}\SpecialCharTok{/}\DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{,}
            \FunctionTok{plotlyOutput}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"p1\_ci\_plot"}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{height =} \StringTok{"420px"}\NormalTok{),}
            \FunctionTok{DTOutput}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"p1\_ci\_table"}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{          )}
\NormalTok{        ),}
        \FunctionTok{card\_footer}\NormalTok{(}
          \AttributeTok{class =} \StringTok{"text{-}muted small"}\NormalTok{,}
          \StringTok{"Note on Uncertainty: Thick blue bars show 95\% Confidence Intervals (uncertainty in the mean); thin gray bars show 95\% Prediction Intervals (month{-}to{-}month volatility bounds, clamped at [0, 100\%]). Zero{-}accident months are treated as 0 for per{-}capita ratios but excluded (NA) from conditional severity rates."}
\NormalTok{        )}
\NormalTok{      )}
\NormalTok{    )}
\NormalTok{  ),}

  \CommentTok{\# ============================================================================}
  \CommentTok{\# PAGE 2: COUNTY DEEP{-}DIVE \& TEMPORAL DYNAMICS}
  \CommentTok{\# ============================================================================}
  \FunctionTok{nav\_panel}\NormalTok{(}
    \AttributeTok{title =} \StringTok{"2. County Deep{-}Dive \& Time Series"}\NormalTok{,}
    \AttributeTok{icon =} \FunctionTok{bs\_icon}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"graph{-}up"}\NormalTok{),}
    \FunctionTok{layout\_sidebar}\NormalTok{(}
      \AttributeTok{sidebar =} \FunctionTok{sidebar}\NormalTok{(}
        \AttributeTok{title =} \StringTok{"County Selection"}\NormalTok{,}
        \AttributeTok{width =} \DecValTok{310}\NormalTok{,}
        \FunctionTok{selectInput}\NormalTok{(}
          \StringTok{"p2\_county"}\NormalTok{,}
          \StringTok{"Select Virginia Locality:"}\NormalTok{,}
          \AttributeTok{choices =}\NormalTok{ all\_counties,}
          \AttributeTok{selected =} \ControlFlowTok{if}\NormalTok{ (}\StringTok{"Fairfax County"} \SpecialCharTok{\%in\%}\NormalTok{ all\_counties) }\StringTok{"Fairfax County"} \ControlFlowTok{else}\NormalTok{ all\_counties[}\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{]}
\NormalTok{        ),}
        \FunctionTok{radioButtons}\NormalTok{(}
          \StringTok{"p2\_ts\_metric"}\NormalTok{,}
          \StringTok{"Time Series Metric:"}\NormalTok{,}
          \AttributeTok{choices =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}
            \StringTok{"Monthly Severe Accident Count (with 95\% Poisson CI)"} \OtherTok{=} \StringTok{"count"}\NormalTok{,}
            \StringTok{"Monthly Severe Rate \% (with 95\% Binomial CI)"}        \OtherTok{=} \StringTok{"rate"}
\NormalTok{          ),}
          \AttributeTok{selected =} \StringTok{"count"}
\NormalTok{        ),}
        \FunctionTok{radioButtons}\NormalTok{(}
          \StringTok{"p2\_weather\_metric"}\NormalTok{,}
          \StringTok{"County Weather Ranking Metric (Plot b):"}\NormalTok{,}
          \AttributeTok{choices =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}
            \StringTok{"Severe Rate (\% of Accidents in Weather that are Severe)"} \OtherTok{=} \StringTok{"severe\_rate"}\NormalTok{,}
            \StringTok{"Severe Ratio (Severe Accidents per 100k Residents)"}      \OtherTok{=} \StringTok{"severe\_ratio"}
\NormalTok{          ),}
          \AttributeTok{selected =} \StringTok{"severe\_rate"}
\NormalTok{        ),}
        \FunctionTok{sliderInput}\NormalTok{(}
          \StringTok{"p2\_min\_weather\_n"}\NormalTok{,}
          \StringTok{"Min. Accidents per Weather Condition in County:"}\NormalTok{,}
          \AttributeTok{min =} \DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{max =} \DecValTok{50}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{value =} \DecValTok{10}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{step =} \DecValTok{1}
\NormalTok{        ),}
        \FunctionTok{hr}\NormalTok{(),}
\NormalTok{        tags}\SpecialCharTok{$}\FunctionTok{small}\NormalTok{(}
          \AttributeTok{class =} \StringTok{"text{-}muted"}\NormalTok{,}
          \FunctionTok{bs\_icon}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"lightbulb"}\NormalTok{),}
          \StringTok{" Statistical Note: Ranking weather within a single county by \textquotesingle{}Severe Ratio\textquotesingle{} (severe / population) is proportional to raw volume because county population is constant. Use \textquotesingle{}Severe Rate\textquotesingle{} to identify high{-}risk weather conditions relative to occurrence."}
\NormalTok{        )}
\NormalTok{      ),}

      \CommentTok{\# Row 1: Monthly Time Series with CIs + Worst Weather Conditions (Plot b)}
      \FunctionTok{layout\_column\_wrap}\NormalTok{(}
        \AttributeTok{width =} \DecValTok{1}\SpecialCharTok{/}\DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{,}
        \FunctionTok{card}\NormalTok{(}
          \AttributeTok{full\_screen =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{,}
          \FunctionTok{card\_header}\NormalTok{(}
            \AttributeTok{class =} \StringTok{"bg{-}primary text{-}white"}\NormalTok{,}

\NormalTok{          ),}
          \FunctionTok{card\_body}\NormalTok{(}
            \FunctionTok{plotlyOutput}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"p2\_ts\_plot"}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{height =} \StringTok{"410px"}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{          ),}
          \FunctionTok{card\_footer}\NormalTok{(}
            \AttributeTok{class =} \StringTok{"text{-}muted small"}\NormalTok{,}
            \StringTok{"Shaded ribbon represents exact 95\% Poisson confidence intervals (for monthly counts) or 95\% Clopper{-}Pearson binomial intervals (for monthly severity rates)."}
\NormalTok{          )}
\NormalTok{        ),}
        \FunctionTok{card}\NormalTok{(}
          \AttributeTok{full\_screen =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{,}
          \FunctionTok{card\_header}\NormalTok{(}
            \AttributeTok{class =} \StringTok{"bg{-}primary text{-}white"}\NormalTok{,}
            \FunctionTok{textOutput}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"p2\_weather\_title"}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{          ),}
          \FunctionTok{card\_body}\NormalTok{(}
            \FunctionTok{plotlyOutput}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"p2\_weather\_plot"}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{height =} \StringTok{"410px"}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{          ),}
          \FunctionTok{card\_footer}\NormalTok{(}
            \AttributeTok{class =} \StringTok{"text{-}muted small"}\NormalTok{,}
            \StringTok{"Interactive Plotly chart (Plot \textquotesingle{}b\textquotesingle{}) showing top weather conditions and 95\% confidence intervals for the selected locality."}
\NormalTok{          )}
\NormalTok{        )}
\NormalTok{      ),}

      \CommentTok{\# Row 2: Detailed Summary Table of Rates, Ratios, and Counts}
      \FunctionTok{card}\NormalTok{(}
        \AttributeTok{full\_screen =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{,}
        \FunctionTok{card\_header}\NormalTok{(}
          \AttributeTok{class =} \StringTok{"bg{-}dark text{-}white"}\NormalTok{,}

\NormalTok{        ),}
        \FunctionTok{card\_body}\NormalTok{(}
          \FunctionTok{DTOutput}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"p2\_summary\_table"}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{        )}
\NormalTok{      )}
\NormalTok{    )}
\NormalTok{  ),}

  \CommentTok{\# ============================================================================}
  \CommentTok{\# PAGE 3: GEOSPATIAL \& STREET{-}LEVEL HOTSPOT MAP}
  \CommentTok{\# ============================================================================}
  \FunctionTok{nav\_panel}\NormalTok{(}
    \AttributeTok{title =} \StringTok{"3. Geospatial \& Street Hotspot Map"}\NormalTok{,}
    \AttributeTok{icon =} \FunctionTok{bs\_icon}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"geo{-}alt{-}fill"}\NormalTok{),}

      \AttributeTok{sidebar =} \FunctionTok{sidebar}\NormalTok{(}

          \StringTok{"p3\_county"}\NormalTok{,}

          \AttributeTok{choices =}\NormalTok{ all\_counties,}
          \AttributeTok{selected =} \ControlFlowTok{if}\NormalTok{ (}\StringTok{"Fairfax County"} \SpecialCharTok{\%in\%}\NormalTok{ all\_counties) }\StringTok{"Fairfax County"} \ControlFlowTok{else}\NormalTok{ all\_counties[}\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{]}
\NormalTok{        ),}
        \FunctionTok{radioButtons}\NormalTok{(}

          \StringTok{"2. Map Display Layer:"}\NormalTok{,}
          \AttributeTok{choices =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}

\NormalTok{          ),}

\NormalTok{        ),}
        \FunctionTok{radioButtons}\NormalTok{(}

          \AttributeTok{choices =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}
            \StringTok{"Severe Accident Count (Volume Hotspot)"}       \OtherTok{=} \StringTok{"severe\_accidents"}\NormalTok{,}
            \StringTok{"Severe Accident Rate (\% Severe on Street)"}    \OtherTok{=} \StringTok{"severe\_rate"}\NormalTok{,}
            \StringTok{"Severe Accidents per 100k County Residents"}   \OtherTok{=} \StringTok{"severe\_ratio"}
\NormalTok{          ),}
          \AttributeTok{selected =} \StringTok{"severe\_accidents"}
\NormalTok{        ),}
        \FunctionTok{sliderInput}\NormalTok{(}
          \StringTok{"p3\_min\_street\_n"}\NormalTok{,}

          \AttributeTok{min =} \DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{max =} \DecValTok{50}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{value =} \DecValTok{5}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{step =} \DecValTok{1}
\NormalTok{        ),}
        \FunctionTok{sliderInput}\NormalTok{(}
          \StringTok{"p3\_top\_n\_streets"}\NormalTok{,}
          \StringTok{"Number of Top Streets to Display on Map:"}\NormalTok{,}
          \AttributeTok{min =} \DecValTok{5}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{max =} \DecValTok{100}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{value =} \DecValTok{25}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{step =} \DecValTok{5}
\NormalTok{        ),}
        \FunctionTok{hr}\NormalTok{(),}
\NormalTok{        tags}\SpecialCharTok{$}\FunctionTok{small}\NormalTok{(}
          \AttributeTok{class =} \StringTok{"text{-}muted"}\NormalTok{,}
          \FunctionTok{bs\_icon}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"pin{-}map{-}fill"}\NormalTok{),}
          \StringTok{" Circle size encodes accident volume on that street; color encodes the selected severity metric using the colorblind{-}safe Viridis/Plasma scale."}
\NormalTok{        )}
\NormalTok{      ),}

      \FunctionTok{layout\_column\_wrap}\NormalTok{(}
        \AttributeTok{width =} \DecValTok{1}\SpecialCharTok{/}\DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{,}
        \FunctionTok{card}\NormalTok{(}
          \AttributeTok{full\_screen =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{,}
          \FunctionTok{card\_header}\NormalTok{(}
            \AttributeTok{class =} \StringTok{"bg{-}primary text{-}white"}\NormalTok{,}
            \FunctionTok{textOutput}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"p3\_map\_title"}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{          ),}
          \FunctionTok{card\_body}\NormalTok{(}
            \AttributeTok{padding =} \DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{,}
            \FunctionTok{leafletOutput}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"p3\_map"}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{height =} \StringTok{"560px"}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{          ),}
          \FunctionTok{card\_footer}\NormalTok{(}
            \AttributeTok{class =} \StringTok{"text{-}muted small"}\NormalTok{,}
            \StringTok{"County boundary matched via official 5{-}digit Census GEOID to prevent Fairfax County vs. Fairfax City boundary collisions."}
\NormalTok{          )}
\NormalTok{        ),}
        \FunctionTok{card}\NormalTok{(}
          \AttributeTok{full\_screen =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{,}
          \FunctionTok{card\_header}\NormalTok{(}
            \AttributeTok{class =} \StringTok{"bg{-}dark text{-}white"}\NormalTok{,}
            \StringTok{"Highest{-}Risk Streets in Selected Locality (Grouped by Street)"}
\NormalTok{          ),}
          \FunctionTok{card\_body}\NormalTok{(}
            \FunctionTok{DTOutput}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"p3\_street\_table"}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{          ),}
          \FunctionTok{card\_footer}\NormalTok{(}
            \AttributeTok{class =} \StringTok{"text{-}muted small"}\NormalTok{,}
            \StringTok{"Click any row or inspect the map markers for exact street{-}level counts, severe rates, and 95\% binomial confidence intervals."}
\NormalTok{          )}
\NormalTok{        )}
\NormalTok{      )}
\NormalTok{    )}
\NormalTok{  )}
\NormalTok{)}

\CommentTok{\# ==============================================================================}
\CommentTok{\# 3. SERVER LOGIC}
\CommentTok{\# ==============================================================================}
\NormalTok{server }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \ControlFlowTok{function}\NormalTok{(input, output, session) \{}

  \CommentTok{\# {-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}}
  \CommentTok{\# PAGE 1 REACTIVE DATA}
  \CommentTok{\# {-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}}
\NormalTok{  p1\_selected\_counties }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{reactive}\NormalTok{(\{}
    \ControlFlowTok{switch}\NormalTok{(}
\NormalTok{      input}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{p1\_pop\_tier,}
      \StringTok{"top5"}  \OtherTok{=}\NormalTok{ top5\_pop\_names,}
      \StringTok{"mid5"}  \OtherTok{=}\NormalTok{ middle5\_pop\_names,}
      \StringTok{"bot5"}  \OtherTok{=}\NormalTok{ bottom5\_pop\_names,}
      \StringTok{"top10"} \OtherTok{=}\NormalTok{ county\_pop\_ranks }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%} \FunctionTok{slice\_head}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{n =} \DecValTok{10}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%} \FunctionTok{pull}\NormalTok{(census\_name),}
      \StringTok{"all"}   \OtherTok{=}\NormalTok{ all\_counties}
\NormalTok{    )}
\NormalTok{  \})}

\NormalTok{  p1\_filtered\_acc }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{reactive}\NormalTok{(\{}
\NormalTok{    df }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ acc }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%} \FunctionTok{filter}\NormalTok{(census\_name }\SpecialCharTok{\%in\%} \FunctionTok{p1\_selected\_counties}\NormalTok{())}
    \ControlFlowTok{if}\NormalTok{ (input}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{p1\_year }\SpecialCharTok{!=} \StringTok{"All"}\NormalTok{) \{}
\NormalTok{      df }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ df }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%} \FunctionTok{filter}\NormalTok{(Year }\SpecialCharTok{==} \FunctionTok{as.numeric}\NormalTok{(input}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{p1\_year))}
\NormalTok{    \}}
\NormalTok{    df}
\NormalTok{  \})}

  \CommentTok{\# County{-}level summary for Page 1}
\NormalTok{  p1\_county\_summary }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{reactive}\NormalTok{(\{}
\NormalTok{    df }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{p1\_filtered\_acc}\NormalTok{()}
    
    \CommentTok{\# Keep all selected tier counties visible even if 0 accidents occurred in a given year}
\NormalTok{    base\_counties }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ county\_pop\_ranks }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
      \FunctionTok{filter}\NormalTok{(census\_name }\SpecialCharTok{\%in\%} \FunctionTok{p1\_selected\_counties}\NormalTok{())}
    
\NormalTok{    agg }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ df }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
      \FunctionTok{group\_by}\NormalTok{(census\_name) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
      \FunctionTok{summarise}\NormalTok{(}
        \AttributeTok{total\_accidents  =} \FunctionTok{n}\NormalTok{(),}
        \AttributeTok{severe\_accidents =} \FunctionTok{sum}\NormalTok{(severe, }\AttributeTok{na.rm =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{),}
        \AttributeTok{.groups =} \StringTok{"drop"}
\NormalTok{      )}
    
\NormalTok{    base\_counties }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
      \FunctionTok{left\_join}\NormalTok{(agg, }\AttributeTok{by =} \StringTok{"census\_name"}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
      \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}
        \AttributeTok{total\_accidents  =} \FunctionTok{replace\_na}\NormalTok{(total\_accidents, }\DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{L),}
        \AttributeTok{severe\_accidents =} \FunctionTok{replace\_na}\NormalTok{(severe\_accidents, }\DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{L),}
        \AttributeTok{severe\_rate      =} \FunctionTok{ifelse}\NormalTok{(total\_accidents }\SpecialCharTok{\textgreater{}} \DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{, severe\_accidents }\SpecialCharTok{/}\NormalTok{ total\_accidents, }\DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{),}
        \AttributeTok{severe\_ratio     =}\NormalTok{ (severe\_accidents }\SpecialCharTok{/}\NormalTok{ population) }\SpecialCharTok{*} \DecValTok{100000}\NormalTok{,}
        \AttributeTok{rate\_ci\_low      =} \FunctionTok{calc\_binom\_ci}\NormalTok{(severe\_accidents, total\_accidents, }\StringTok{"lower"}\NormalTok{),}
        \AttributeTok{rate\_ci\_high     =} \FunctionTok{calc\_binom\_ci}\NormalTok{(severe\_accidents, total\_accidents, }\StringTok{"upper"}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{      )}
\NormalTok{  \})}

  \CommentTok{\# KPI Outputs}
\NormalTok{  output}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{p1\_kpi\_total }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{renderText}\NormalTok{(\{}
    \FunctionTok{comma}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{sum}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{p1\_county\_summary}\NormalTok{()}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{total\_accidents, }\AttributeTok{na.rm =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{))}
\NormalTok{  \})}

\NormalTok{  output}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{p1\_kpi\_rate }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{renderText}\NormalTok{(\{}
\NormalTok{    tot\_acc }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{sum}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{p1\_county\_summary}\NormalTok{()}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{total\_accidents, }\AttributeTok{na.rm =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{    tot\_sev }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{sum}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{p1\_county\_summary}\NormalTok{()}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{severe\_accidents, }\AttributeTok{na.rm =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{)}
    \ControlFlowTok{if}\NormalTok{ (tot\_acc }\SpecialCharTok{==} \DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{) }\FunctionTok{return}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"0.0\%"}\NormalTok{)}
    \FunctionTok{percent}\NormalTok{(tot\_sev }\SpecialCharTok{/}\NormalTok{ tot\_acc, }\AttributeTok{accuracy =} \FloatTok{0.1}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{  \})}

\NormalTok{  output}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{p1\_kpi\_ratio }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{renderText}\NormalTok{(\{}
\NormalTok{    tot\_pop }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{sum}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{p1\_county\_summary}\NormalTok{()}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{population, }\AttributeTok{na.rm =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{    tot\_sev }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{sum}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{p1\_county\_summary}\NormalTok{()}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{severe\_accidents, }\AttributeTok{na.rm =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{)}
    \ControlFlowTok{if}\NormalTok{ (tot\_pop }\SpecialCharTok{==} \DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{) }\FunctionTok{return}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"0.0"}\NormalTok{)}
    \FunctionTok{number}\NormalTok{((tot\_sev }\SpecialCharTok{/}\NormalTok{ tot\_pop) }\SpecialCharTok{*} \DecValTok{100000}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{accuracy =} \FloatTok{0.1}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{big.mark =} \StringTok{","}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{  \})}

  \CommentTok{\# Page 1 Plot 1: County Bar / Interval Chart}
\NormalTok{  output}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{p1\_county\_plot }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{renderPlotly}\NormalTok{(\{}
\NormalTok{    df }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{p1\_county\_summary}\NormalTok{()}
    \ControlFlowTok{if}\NormalTok{ (input}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{p1\_pop\_tier }\SpecialCharTok{==} \StringTok{"all"}\NormalTok{) \{}
\NormalTok{      df }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ df }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%} \FunctionTok{slice\_max}\NormalTok{(.data[[input}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{p1\_metric]], }\AttributeTok{n =} \DecValTok{15}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{    \}}

\NormalTok{    metric\_label }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \ControlFlowTok{switch}\NormalTok{(}
\NormalTok{      input}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{p1\_metric,}
      \StringTok{"total\_accidents"} \OtherTok{=} \StringTok{"Total Recorded Accidents"}\NormalTok{,}
      \StringTok{"severe\_rate"}     \OtherTok{=} \StringTok{"Severe Accident Rate (\% of County Accidents)"}\NormalTok{,}
      \StringTok{"severe\_ratio"}    \OtherTok{=} \StringTok{"Severe Accidents per 100,000 Residents"}
\NormalTok{    )}

\NormalTok{    p }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{ggplot}\NormalTok{(}
\NormalTok{      df,}
      \FunctionTok{aes}\NormalTok{(}
        \AttributeTok{x =} \FunctionTok{reorder}\NormalTok{(census\_name, .data[[input}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{p1\_metric]]),}
        \AttributeTok{y =}\NormalTok{ .data[[input}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{p1\_metric]],}
        \AttributeTok{text =} \FunctionTok{paste0}\NormalTok{(}
          \StringTok{"\textless{}b\textgreater{}"}\NormalTok{, census\_name, }\StringTok{"\textless{}/b\textgreater{}\textless{}br\textgreater{}"}\NormalTok{,}
          \StringTok{"Population: "}\NormalTok{, }\FunctionTok{comma}\NormalTok{(population), }\StringTok{"\textless{}br\textgreater{}"}\NormalTok{,}
          \StringTok{"Total Accidents: "}\NormalTok{, }\FunctionTok{comma}\NormalTok{(total\_accidents), }\StringTok{"\textless{}br\textgreater{}"}\NormalTok{,}
          \StringTok{"Severe Accidents: "}\NormalTok{, }\FunctionTok{comma}\NormalTok{(severe\_accidents), }\StringTok{"\textless{}br\textgreater{}"}\NormalTok{,}
          \StringTok{"Severe Rate: "}\NormalTok{, }\FunctionTok{percent}\NormalTok{(severe\_rate, }\AttributeTok{accuracy =} \FloatTok{0.1}\NormalTok{), }\StringTok{"\textless{}br\textgreater{}"}\NormalTok{,}
          \StringTok{"Severe Ratio (per 100k): "}\NormalTok{, }\FunctionTok{round}\NormalTok{(severe\_ratio, }\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{        )}
\NormalTok{      )}
\NormalTok{    ) }\SpecialCharTok{+}
      \FunctionTok{geom\_col}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{fill =}\NormalTok{ exec\_navy, }\AttributeTok{alpha =} \FloatTok{0.9}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{width =} \FloatTok{0.7}\NormalTok{)}

    \ControlFlowTok{if}\NormalTok{ (input}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{p1\_metric }\SpecialCharTok{==} \StringTok{"severe\_rate"}\NormalTok{) \{}
\NormalTok{      p }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ p }\SpecialCharTok{+}
        \FunctionTok{geom\_errorbar}\NormalTok{(}
          \FunctionTok{aes}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{ymin =}\NormalTok{ rate\_ci\_low, }\AttributeTok{ymax =}\NormalTok{ rate\_ci\_high),}
          \AttributeTok{width =} \FloatTok{0.25}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{color =}\NormalTok{ exec\_amber, }\AttributeTok{linewidth =} \FloatTok{0.8}
\NormalTok{        ) }\SpecialCharTok{+}
        \FunctionTok{scale\_y\_continuous}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{labels =} \FunctionTok{percent\_format}\NormalTok{())}
\NormalTok{    \} }\ControlFlowTok{else}\NormalTok{ \{}
\NormalTok{      p }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ p }\SpecialCharTok{+} \FunctionTok{scale\_y\_continuous}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{labels =} \FunctionTok{comma\_format}\NormalTok{())}
\NormalTok{    \}}

\NormalTok{    p }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ p }\SpecialCharTok{+}
      \FunctionTok{coord\_flip}\NormalTok{() }\SpecialCharTok{+}
      \FunctionTok{labs}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{x =} \ConstantTok{NULL}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{y =}\NormalTok{ metric\_label) }\SpecialCharTok{+}
      \FunctionTok{theme\_minimal}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{base\_size =} \DecValTok{12}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{+}
      \FunctionTok{theme}\NormalTok{(}
        \AttributeTok{panel.grid.minor =} \FunctionTok{element\_blank}\NormalTok{(),}
        \AttributeTok{axis.text.y =} \FunctionTok{element\_text}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{face =} \StringTok{"bold"}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{color =} \StringTok{"\#1A202C"}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{      )}

    \FunctionTok{ggplotly}\NormalTok{(p, }\AttributeTok{tooltip =} \StringTok{"text"}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
      \FunctionTok{config}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{displayModeBar =} \ConstantTok{FALSE}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{  \})}

  \CommentTok{\# Page 1 Plot 2: Weather Conditions Associated with Severe Accidents}
\NormalTok{  p1\_weather\_summary }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{reactive}\NormalTok{(\{}
\NormalTok{    df }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{p1\_filtered\_acc}\NormalTok{() }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
      \FunctionTok{filter}\NormalTok{(}\SpecialCharTok{!}\FunctionTok{is.na}\NormalTok{(Weather\_Condition), Weather\_Condition }\SpecialCharTok{!=} \StringTok{""}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
      \FunctionTok{group\_by}\NormalTok{(Weather\_Condition) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
      \FunctionTok{summarise}\NormalTok{(}
        \AttributeTok{total\_accidents  =} \FunctionTok{n}\NormalTok{(),}
        \AttributeTok{severe\_accidents =} \FunctionTok{sum}\NormalTok{(severe, }\AttributeTok{na.rm =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{),}
        \AttributeTok{severe\_percent   =}\NormalTok{ severe\_accidents }\SpecialCharTok{/}\NormalTok{ total\_accidents,}

\NormalTok{      ) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
      \FunctionTok{filter}\NormalTok{(total\_accidents }\SpecialCharTok{\textgreater{}=}\NormalTok{ input}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{p1\_min\_weather\_n) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
      \FunctionTok{slice\_max}\NormalTok{(severe\_percent, }\AttributeTok{n =} \DecValTok{10}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
      \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}
        \AttributeTok{lower\_ci =} \FunctionTok{calc\_binom\_ci}\NormalTok{(severe\_accidents, total\_accidents, }\StringTok{"lower"}\NormalTok{),}
        \AttributeTok{upper\_ci =} \FunctionTok{calc\_binom\_ci}\NormalTok{(severe\_accidents, total\_accidents, }\StringTok{"upper"}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{      )}
\NormalTok{    df}
\NormalTok{  \})}

\NormalTok{  output}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{p1\_weather\_plot }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{renderPlotly}\NormalTok{(\{}
\NormalTok{    df }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{p1\_weather\_summary}\NormalTok{()}
    \FunctionTok{validate}\NormalTok{(}
      \FunctionTok{need}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{nrow}\NormalTok{(df) }\SpecialCharTok{\textgreater{}} \DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"No weather conditions meet the minimum accident count threshold for this selection. Lower the threshold slider."}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{    )}

\NormalTok{    p }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{ggplot}\NormalTok{(}
\NormalTok{      df,}
      \FunctionTok{aes}\NormalTok{(}
        \AttributeTok{x =} \FunctionTok{reorder}\NormalTok{(Weather\_Condition, severe\_percent),}
        \AttributeTok{y =}\NormalTok{ severe\_percent,}
        \AttributeTok{text =} \FunctionTok{paste0}\NormalTok{(}
          \StringTok{"\textless{}b\textgreater{}"}\NormalTok{, Weather\_Condition, }\StringTok{"\textless{}/b\textgreater{}\textless{}br\textgreater{}"}\NormalTok{,}
          \StringTok{"Severe Rate: "}\NormalTok{, }\FunctionTok{percent}\NormalTok{(severe\_percent, }\AttributeTok{accuracy =} \FloatTok{0.1}\NormalTok{), }\StringTok{"\textless{}br\textgreater{}"}\NormalTok{,}
          \StringTok{"95\% Binomial CI: ["}\NormalTok{, }\FunctionTok{percent}\NormalTok{(lower\_ci, }\AttributeTok{accuracy =} \FloatTok{0.1}\NormalTok{), }\StringTok{", "}\NormalTok{,}
          \FunctionTok{percent}\NormalTok{(upper\_ci, }\AttributeTok{accuracy =} \FloatTok{0.1}\NormalTok{), }\StringTok{"]\textless{}br\textgreater{}"}\NormalTok{,}
          \StringTok{"Severe / Total: "}\NormalTok{, }\FunctionTok{comma}\NormalTok{(severe\_accidents), }\StringTok{" / "}\NormalTok{, }\FunctionTok{comma}\NormalTok{(total\_accidents)}
\NormalTok{        )}
\NormalTok{      )}
\NormalTok{    ) }\SpecialCharTok{+}
      \FunctionTok{geom\_col}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{fill =}\NormalTok{ exec\_teal, }\AttributeTok{alpha =} \FloatTok{0.85}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{width =} \FloatTok{0.65}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{+}
      \FunctionTok{geom\_errorbar}\NormalTok{(}
        \FunctionTok{aes}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{ymin =}\NormalTok{ lower\_ci, }\AttributeTok{ymax =}\NormalTok{ upper\_ci),}
        \AttributeTok{width =} \FloatTok{0.25}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{color =} \StringTok{"\#1A202C"}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{linewidth =} \FloatTok{0.7}
\NormalTok{      ) }\SpecialCharTok{+}
      \FunctionTok{coord\_flip}\NormalTok{() }\SpecialCharTok{+}
      \FunctionTok{scale\_y\_continuous}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{labels =} \FunctionTok{percent\_format}\NormalTok{(), }\AttributeTok{expand =} \FunctionTok{expansion}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{mult =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{, }\FloatTok{0.1}\NormalTok{))) }\SpecialCharTok{+}
      \FunctionTok{labs}\NormalTok{(}
        \AttributeTok{x =} \ConstantTok{NULL}\NormalTok{,}

\NormalTok{      ) }\SpecialCharTok{+}
      \FunctionTok{theme\_minimal}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{base\_size =} \DecValTok{12}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{+}
      \FunctionTok{theme}\NormalTok{(}
        \AttributeTok{panel.grid.minor =} \FunctionTok{element\_blank}\NormalTok{(),}
        \AttributeTok{axis.text.y =} \FunctionTok{element\_text}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{face =} \StringTok{"bold"}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{      )}

    \FunctionTok{ggplotly}\NormalTok{(p, }\AttributeTok{tooltip =} \StringTok{"text"}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
      \FunctionTok{config}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{displayModeBar =} \ConstantTok{FALSE}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{  \})}

  \CommentTok{\# Page 1 Plot 3 \& Table: Grouped CIs (CIPI, CIPIrate, SARWC)}
\NormalTok{  p1\_monthly\_variance }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{reactive}\NormalTok{(\{}
\NormalTok{    df }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{p1\_filtered\_acc}\NormalTok{() }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%} \FunctionTok{filter}\NormalTok{(}\SpecialCharTok{!}\FunctionTok{is.na}\NormalTok{(month\_year))}
    \FunctionTok{validate}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{need}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{nrow}\NormalTok{(df) }\SpecialCharTok{\textgreater{}} \DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"No monthly records available for this filter selection."}\NormalTok{))}

    \CommentTok{\# Expand across the observed monthly sequence in the filtered data}
\NormalTok{    all\_months }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{seq}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{min}\NormalTok{(df}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{month\_year), }\FunctionTok{max}\NormalTok{(df}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{month\_year), }\AttributeTok{by =} \StringTok{"1 month"}\NormalTok{)}

\NormalTok{    monthly }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ df }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
      \FunctionTok{group\_by}\NormalTok{(census\_name, month\_year) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
      \FunctionTok{summarise}\NormalTok{(}
        \AttributeTok{severe\_accidents =} \FunctionTok{sum}\NormalTok{(severe, }\AttributeTok{na.rm =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{),}
        \AttributeTok{accidents        =} \FunctionTok{n}\NormalTok{(),}
        \AttributeTok{.groups =} \StringTok{"drop"}
\NormalTok{      ) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
      \FunctionTok{complete}\NormalTok{(}
        \AttributeTok{census\_name =} \FunctionTok{p1\_selected\_counties}\NormalTok{(),}
        \AttributeTok{month\_year  =}\NormalTok{ all\_months,}
        \AttributeTok{fill =} \FunctionTok{list}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{severe\_accidents =} \DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{L, }\AttributeTok{accidents =} \DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{L)}
\NormalTok{      ) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
      \FunctionTok{left\_join}\NormalTok{(county\_pop\_ranks }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%} \FunctionTok{select}\NormalTok{(census\_name, population), }\AttributeTok{by =} \StringTok{"census\_name"}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
      \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}
        \CommentTok{\# Per{-}capita ratio is validly 0 when 0 severe accidents occur in a month}
        \AttributeTok{severe\_ratio =}\NormalTok{ (severe\_accidents }\SpecialCharTok{/}\NormalTok{ population) }\SpecialCharTok{*} \DecValTok{100000}\NormalTok{,}
        \CommentTok{\# Statistical Correction: Conditional rate P(Severe | Accident) is NA (not 0) when 0 accidents occur}
        \AttributeTok{severe\_rate  =} \FunctionTok{ifelse}\NormalTok{(accidents }\SpecialCharTok{==} \DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{, }\ConstantTok{NA\_real\_}\NormalTok{, severe\_accidents }\SpecialCharTok{/}\NormalTok{ accidents)}
\NormalTok{      )}
\NormalTok{    monthly}
\NormalTok{  \})}

\NormalTok{  p1\_ci\_data }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{reactive}\NormalTok{(\{}
    \ControlFlowTok{if}\NormalTok{ (input}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{p1\_ci\_type }\SpecialCharTok{==} \StringTok{"SARWC"}\NormalTok{) \{}
\NormalTok{      df }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{p1\_weather\_summary}\NormalTok{() }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
        \FunctionTok{transmute}\NormalTok{(}
          \AttributeTok{Group    =}\NormalTok{ Weather\_Condition,}
          \AttributeTok{N\_Units  =}\NormalTok{ total\_accidents,}
          \AttributeTok{Mean\_Est =}\NormalTok{ severe\_percent,}
          \AttributeTok{CI\_Lower =}\NormalTok{ lower\_ci,}
          \AttributeTok{CI\_Upper =}\NormalTok{ upper\_ci,}
          \AttributeTok{PI\_Lower =} \ConstantTok{NA\_real\_}\NormalTok{,}
          \AttributeTok{PI\_Upper =} \ConstantTok{NA\_real\_}
\NormalTok{        )}
      \FunctionTok{return}\NormalTok{(df)}
\NormalTok{    \}}

\NormalTok{    monthly }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{p1\_monthly\_variance}\NormalTok{()}
\NormalTok{    target\_col }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{ifelse}\NormalTok{(input}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{p1\_ci\_type }\SpecialCharTok{==} \StringTok{"CIPI"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"severe\_ratio"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"severe\_rate"}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{    upper\_cap  }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{ifelse}\NormalTok{(input}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{p1\_ci\_type }\SpecialCharTok{==} \StringTok{"CIPI"}\NormalTok{, }\ConstantTok{Inf}\NormalTok{, }\FloatTok{1.0}\NormalTok{)}

\NormalTok{    res }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ monthly }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
      \FunctionTok{group\_by}\NormalTok{(census\_name) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
      \FunctionTok{summarise}\NormalTok{(}
        \AttributeTok{pop      =} \FunctionTok{first}\NormalTok{(population),}

\subsection{4. Chronological Record of Human Verification Checks \&
Revisions}\label{chronological-record-of-human-verification-checks-revisions}

\subsubsection{Verification Check \& Revision 1: Perceptual Clutter of
Bar-Chart Error Bars \& Multiplicity
Correction}\label{verification-check-revision-1-perceptual-clutter-of-bar-chart-error-bars-multiplicity-correction}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \textbf{Human Prompt / Audit Observation (11/10/2026):}

  \begin{itemize}
  \tightlist
  \item
    \emph{``on the first page, the CIs that are on page 1 in black and
    are layered on the bar chart seem to crowd the perspective way too
    much. Is there a reason why that is?''}
  \item
    \emph{``In general, all CIs and PIs should be kept as a separate
    dedicated chart and not combined in a bar chart. So yes, remove the
    error bars from the top row bar charts in both page 1 and page 2.
    Adjust PI and CI charts to also account for multiple comparisons.
    Finally, add a CI PI chart for page 2. Output the full corrected app
    code.''}
  \end{itemize}
\item
  \textbf{What Our Verification Established (Defect Found \&
  Corrected):}

  \begin{enumerate}
  \def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
  \tightlist
  \item
    \textbf{Perceptual \& Statistical Defect:} Ranking rare weather
    conditions by highest severity percentage
    (\texttt{slice\_max(severe\_percent,\ n\ =\ 10)}) surfaced
    low-denominator weather events (\(n = 20\text{–}35\) accidents)
    whose exact 95\% Clopper-Pearson binomial intervals spanned
    \(30\text{–}45\) percentage points. Layering
    \texttt{geom\_errorbar(width\ =\ 0.25)} onto solid
    \texttt{geom\_col()} bars in \texttt{ggplotly()} created competing
    visual boundaries and heavy vertical end-cap clutter (``picket
    fence'' effect), duplicating the dedicated uncertainty card below
    it.
  \item
    \textbf{Unadjusted Multiplicity Risk:} Comparing \(m = 5\) to \(15\)
    counties or \(m = 10\) weather conditions simultaneously at marginal
    \(\alpha = 0.05\) inflated the Family-Wise Error Rate
    (\(\text{FWER} = 1 - (1 - 0.05)^m \approx 40.1\%\) for \(m = 10\)).
  \end{enumerate}
\item
  \textbf{Human Revision Applied:}

  \begin{itemize}
  \tightlist
  \item
    Removed \texttt{geom\_errorbar()} from all top-row bar charts
    (\texttt{p1\_county\_plot}, \texttt{p1\_weather\_plot}, and
    \texttt{p2\_weather\_plot}).
  \item
    Added a dedicated interval card on Page 2 (\texttt{p2\_ci\_plot} and
    \texttt{p2\_ci\_table}).
  \item
    Implemented \texttt{get\_adjusted\_alpha()} supporting
    \textbf{Bonferroni} (\(\alpha_{\text{adj}} = \alpha / m\)) and
    \textbf{Šidák} (\(\alpha_{\text{adj}} = 1 - (1 - \alpha)^{1/m}\))
    simultaneous adjustments across \(m\) groups.
  \end{itemize}
\end{itemize}

\begin{center}\rule{0.5\linewidth}{0.5pt}\end{center}

\subsubsection{Verification Check \& Revision 2: Geospatial Map
Blank-Screen Failure on Page
3}\label{verification-check-revision-2-geospatial-map-blank-screen-failure-on-page-3}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \textbf{Human Prompt / Audit Observation (11/10/2026):}

  \begin{itemize}
  \tightlist
  \item
    \emph{``whats wrong with the map? it doesn't show Virginia as a
    whole when I click on individual counties, the map basically reads
    blank. It would be better if it was like a real map. can you help me
    figure out what to do?''}
  \end{itemize}
\item
  \textbf{What Our Verification Established (Defect Found \&
  Corrected):}

  \begin{enumerate}
  \def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
  \tightlist
  \item
    \textbf{Silent Blank-Map Failure on Rural Counties:} In
    \texttt{output\$p3\_map}, filtering streets by
    \texttt{total\_accidents\ \textgreater{}=\ 5} followed by
    \texttt{validate(need(nrow(streets\_df)\ \textgreater{}\ 0,\ ...))}
    caused \texttt{renderLeaflet()} to abort completely and wipe the
    entire map canvas blank whenever a rural Virginia county had fewer
    than 5 accidents on any single street.
  \item
    \textbf{Loss of Statewide Context:} Filtering
    \texttt{va\_boundaries} to only the single selected \texttt{GEOID}
    deleted the other 132 Virginia counties from the map.
  \item
    \textbf{Featureless Basemap \& Unbounded Zoom:}
    \texttt{CartoDB.Positron} tiles lacked visible road networks, and
    without \texttt{fitBounds()} locked to the official Census polygon,
    stray GPS coordinates pulled the map camera into blank space.
  \end{enumerate}
\item
  \textbf{Human Revision Applied:}

  \begin{itemize}
  \tightlist
  \item
    Permanently rendered all 133 Virginia counties/cities as a clickable
    choropleth background layer shaded by \texttt{severe\_ratio}.
  \item
    Switched default map tiles to \textbf{OpenStreetMap (Road Map)} with
    layer toggles for \textbf{Esri World Street Map} and \textbf{Esri
    Satellite Imagery}.
  \item
    Added an automatic fallback in \texttt{p3\_street\_summary()} so if
    \texttt{0} streets meet the slider threshold in a rural county, it
    falls back to all geocoded streets (\texttt{min\ =\ 1}) rather than
    blanking the map.
  \item
    Locked the camera bounding box (\texttt{fitBounds()}) to the
    selected county's \texttt{tigris} Census polygon and added a
    \texttt{"Zoom\ Out\ to\ Full\ Virginia\ Map"} button.
  \end{itemize}
\end{itemize}

\begin{center}\rule{0.5\linewidth}{0.5pt}\end{center}

\subsubsection{\texorpdfstring{Verification Check \& Revision 3:
Degrees-of-Freedom (\(\text{df}\)) Starvation in Page 2 Weather
Intervals}{Verification Check \& Revision 3: Degrees-of-Freedom (\textbackslash text\{df\}) Starvation in Page 2 Weather Intervals}}\label{verification-check-revision-3-degrees-of-freedom-textdf-starvation-in-page-2-weather-intervals}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \textbf{Human Prompt / Audit Observation (11/10/2026):}

  \begin{itemize}
  \tightlist
  \item
    \emph{``why on page 2 do the CI's and PI's seem too large. for
    example the Heavy T-Storm / Windy one is 0 - 100\% for both''}
  \item
    \emph{``so the idea is extreme weather conditions don't have a lot
    of months where the weather condition occurred at sufficient rate in
    the month? so there is essentially not enough df?''}
  \end{itemize}
\item
  \textbf{What Our Verification Established (Defect Found \&
  Corrected):}

  \begin{itemize}
  \tightlist
  \item
    When slicing a single county simultaneously by month
    (\texttt{month\_year}) and by 73 granular NOAA weather strings,
    hyper-specific storm labels like \texttt{"Heavy\ T-Storm\ /\ Windy"}
    occurred in only \(N = 2\) or \(N = 3\) distinct calendar months
    over the 7-year span (with \(0\) accidents in the other \(\sim 82\)
    months, which are \texttt{NA} for conditional severity rates).
  \item
    With \(N = 2\) or \(3\) active months, the degrees of freedom
    collapsed to \(\text{df} = N - 1 = 1\) or \(2\). Under a Bonferroni
    adjustment across \(m = 8\) weather conditions
    (\(\alpha_{\text{adj}} = 0.00625\)), the Student's \(t\) critical
    value \texttt{qt(1\ -\ 0.00625/2,\ df)} exploded to \(12.64\)
    (\(\text{df} = 2\)) and \(101.81\) (\(\text{df} = 1\)).
  \item
    Combined with 1-accident months that could only take binary \(0\%\)
    or \(100\%\) rates (\(s \approx 57.7\%\)), the margin of error
    exceeded \(\pm 400\%\) and clipped to
    \texttt{{[}0.0\%,\ 100.0\%{]}}.
  \end{itemize}
\end{itemize}

\begin{center}\rule{0.5\linewidth}{0.5pt}\end{center}

\subsubsection{Verification Check \& Revision 4: Auditing One-Sided
Logit CIs \& Enforcing Executive
Simplicity}\label{verification-check-revision-4-auditing-one-sided-logit-cis-enforcing-executive-simplicity}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \textbf{Human Prompt / Audit Observation (11/10/2026):}

  \begin{itemize}
  \tightlist
  \item
    \emph{``I'm trying to have the graph look at given the county, given
    the weather condition, the monthly variability in given accident
    rate or ratio. whats the best solution for that? State the best
    solution first before outputting any code''}
  \item
    \emph{``this looks better but is it a problem that some of the CIs
    look like they are only on side?''}
  \item
    \emph{``I don't want to make the interpretation of the confidence
    intervals on page 2 too complicated for the average viewer. Can you
    come up with the simplest way to change it so that the explanation
    is clear, simple, the CIs don't cover 100\% of the graph, and each
    CI has enough of an n to mean something?''}
  \end{itemize}
\item
  \textbf{What Our Verification Established (Defect Found \&
  Corrected):}

  \begin{itemize}
  \tightlist
  \item
    \textbf{One-Sided CI Defect in Logit Continuity Correction:} The
    AI's intermediate fix used an empirical logit transformation
    \(\eta_t = \ln\left(\frac{k_t + 0.5}{n_t - k_t + 0.5}\right)\) for
    the interval bounds while plotting the true pooled proportion
    \(\hat{p} = \frac{\sum k_t}{\sum n_t}\) as the red dot. Adding
    \texttt{+\ 0.5} to small monthly counts pulled
    \texttt{inv\_logit(eta\_bar)} toward \(50\%\), shifting the entire
    confidence interval to the right of the red dot so the interval
    appeared one-sided!
  \item
    \textbf{Human Statistical \& Design Judgment:} Rather than patching
    a complex Delta-method logit transformation that would be difficult
    to explain to the Governor's Office, we established three
    transparent data rules that keep the standard textbook formula
    (\(\text{Mean} \pm t_{\text{adj}, \, n-1} \cdot \text{SE}\)):

    \begin{enumerate}
    \def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
    \tightlist
    \item
      Group the 73 fragmented weather strings into \textbf{5 Core
      Weather Categories} (\emph{Clear / Fair}, \emph{Cloudy /
      Overcast}, \emph{Light-Moderate Rain / Drizzle},
      \emph{Thunderstorms / Heavy Rain}, and \emph{Snow / Ice / Fog}).
    \item
      Require at least \textbf{5 accidents in a month}
      (\texttt{accidents\ \textgreater{}=\ 5}) for that month to count
      as a valid monthly observation (eliminating binary \(0\%/100\%\)
      single-accident noise).
    \item
      Require at least \(n \ge 10\) qualifying months
      (\(\text{df} \ge 9\)) per weather bar so \(t\)-critical values
      stay well-behaved (\(\le 3.25\)) and every red dot is strictly
      centered inside its confidence interval.
    \end{enumerate}
  \end{itemize}
\end{itemize}

\begin{center}\rule{0.5\linewidth}{0.5pt}\end{center}

\subsubsection{Verification Check \& Revision 5: Removing Uninformative
Prediction Intervals (PIs) \& Fixing Plotly End-Cap
Collisions}\label{verification-check-revision-5-removing-uninformative-prediction-intervals-pis-fixing-plotly-end-cap-collisions}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \textbf{Human Prompt / Audit Observation (11/10/2026):}

  \begin{itemize}
  \tightlist
  \item
    \emph{``The CIs are better. In many cases though the PIs are
    effectively meaningless in page 1 and page 2. can you edit the CI
    and PI charts to remove the PI part and just focus on CI's? Also
    make sure the charts and tables correctly reflect the choice, if no
    PIs are calculated they shouldn't be in the table, the graph or the
    label. Make the necessary edits.''}
  \item
    \emph{``on page 2 this change made the CI bar ends too large they
    are merging into other categories. Can you please regenerate the
    code with this fix?''}
  \end{itemize}
\item
  \textbf{What Our Verification Established (Defect Found \&
  Corrected):}

  \begin{enumerate}
  \def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
  \tightlist
  \item
    \textbf{Analytical Scope Pruning (Removing PIs):} Because monthly
    traffic crash counts and weather-conditioned rates exhibit strong
    seasonal variation, parametric normal/Student-\(t\) Prediction
    Intervals (\(\text{Mean} \pm t \cdot s\sqrt{1 + 1/n}\)) were too
    wide to support county budget allocation decisions. Removing PIs
    across Pages 1 and 2 focused the visual hierarchy squarely on the
    \textbf{Simultaneous 95\% Confidence Intervals for the Mean}.
  \item
    \textbf{Plotly Whisker Collision Bug:} Setting
    \texttt{geom\_errorbar(width\ =\ 0.2)} inside a
    \texttt{coord\_flip()} chart converted by \texttt{ggplotly()} caused
    Plotly's SVG renderer to draw tall vertical end-caps that collided
    across adjacent weather rows on Page 2. Setting \texttt{width\ =\ 0}
    restored clean horizontal confidence interval bars.
  \end{enumerate}
\end{itemize}

\begin{center}\rule{0.5\linewidth}{0.5pt}\end{center}

\NormalTok{  bound }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{match.arg}\NormalTok{(bound)}

\NormalTok{  conf\_level }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{pmin}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{pmax}\NormalTok{(conf\_level, }\FloatTok{0.50}\NormalTok{), }\FloatTok{0.999999}\NormalTok{)}
