    \FunctionTok{binom.test}\NormalTok{(k, total, }\AttributeTok{conf.level =}\NormalTok{ conf\_level)}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{conf.int[idx]}
\NormalTok{  \})}
\NormalTok{\}}

\NormalTok{calc\_poisson\_ci }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \ControlFlowTok{function}\NormalTok{(k\_vec, }\AttributeTok{bound =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"lower"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"upper"}\NormalTok{), }\AttributeTok{conf\_level =} \FloatTok{0.95}\NormalTok{) \{}
\NormalTok{  bound }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{match.arg}\NormalTok{(bound)}
\NormalTok{  idx }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{ifelse}\NormalTok{(bound }\SpecialCharTok{==} \StringTok{"lower"}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{  conf\_level }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{pmin}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{pmax}\NormalTok{(conf\_level, }\FloatTok{0.50}\NormalTok{), }\FloatTok{0.999999}\NormalTok{)}
  \FunctionTok{map\_dbl}\NormalTok{(k\_vec, }\ControlFlowTok{function}\NormalTok{(k) \{}
    \ControlFlowTok{if}\NormalTok{ (}\FunctionTok{is.na}\NormalTok{(k) }\SpecialCharTok{||}\NormalTok{ k }\SpecialCharTok{\textless{}} \DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{) }\FunctionTok{return}\NormalTok{(}\ConstantTok{NA\_real\_}\NormalTok{)}
    \FunctionTok{poisson.test}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{as.integer}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{round}\NormalTok{(k)), }\AttributeTok{conf.level =}\NormalTok{ conf\_level)}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{conf.int[idx]}
\NormalTok{  \})}
\NormalTok{\}}

\NormalTok{compute\_simple\_ci }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \ControlFlowTok{function}\NormalTok{(df, group\_col, value\_col, }\AttributeTok{pop\_col =} \ConstantTok{NULL}\NormalTok{,}
                              \AttributeTok{mult\_method =} \StringTok{"bonferroni"}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{upper\_cap =} \ConstantTok{Inf}\NormalTok{,}
                              \AttributeTok{min\_months =} \DecValTok{10}\NormalTok{) \{}
\NormalTok{  grouped }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ df }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{filter}\NormalTok{(}\SpecialCharTok{!}\FunctionTok{is.na}\NormalTok{(.data[[value\_col]]) }\SpecialCharTok{\&} \SpecialCharTok{!}\FunctionTok{is.nan}\NormalTok{(.data[[value\_col]])) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{group\_by}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{Group =}\NormalTok{ .data[[group\_col]]) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{summarise}\NormalTok{(}
      \AttributeTok{pop      =} \ControlFlowTok{if}\NormalTok{ (}\SpecialCharTok{!}\FunctionTok{is.null}\NormalTok{(pop\_col)) }\FunctionTok{first}\NormalTok{(.data[[pop\_col]]) }\ControlFlowTok{else} \ConstantTok{NA\_real\_}\NormalTok{,}
      \AttributeTok{N\_Units  =} \FunctionTok{n}\NormalTok{(),}
      \AttributeTok{Mean\_Est =} \FunctionTok{mean}\NormalTok{(.data[[value\_col]], }\AttributeTok{na.rm =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{),}
      \AttributeTok{variance =} \FunctionTok{var}\NormalTok{(.data[[value\_col]], }\AttributeTok{na.rm =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{),}
      \AttributeTok{stdev    =} \FunctionTok{sqrt}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{pmax}\NormalTok{(}\DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{, variance)),}
      \AttributeTok{SE       =}\NormalTok{ stdev }\SpecialCharTok{/} \FunctionTok{sqrt}\NormalTok{(N\_Units),}
      \AttributeTok{.groups  =} \StringTok{"drop"}
\NormalTok{    ) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{filter}\NormalTok{(Mean\_Est }\SpecialCharTok{\textgreater{}} \DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{)}

\NormalTok{  filtered }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ grouped }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%} \FunctionTok{filter}\NormalTok{(N\_Units }\SpecialCharTok{\textgreater{}=}\NormalTok{ min\_months)}
  \ControlFlowTok{if}\NormalTok{ (}\FunctionTok{nrow}\NormalTok{(filtered) }\SpecialCharTok{==} \DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{) filtered }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ grouped }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%} \FunctionTok{filter}\NormalTok{(N\_Units }\SpecialCharTok{\textgreater{}=} \DecValTok{5}\NormalTok{)}
  \ControlFlowTok{if}\NormalTok{ (}\FunctionTok{nrow}\NormalTok{(filtered) }\SpecialCharTok{==} \DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{) filtered }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ grouped }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%} \FunctionTok{filter}\NormalTok{(N\_Units }\SpecialCharTok{\textgreater{}=} \DecValTok{3}\NormalTok{)}

\NormalTok{  m\_groups  }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{max}\NormalTok{(}\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{, }\FunctionTok{nrow}\NormalTok{(filtered))}
\NormalTok{  alpha\_adj }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{get\_adjusted\_alpha}\NormalTok{(m\_groups, mult\_method, }\AttributeTok{alpha =} \FloatTok{0.05}\NormalTok{)}

\NormalTok{  filtered }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}
      \AttributeTok{m\_comparisons =}\NormalTok{ m\_groups,}
      \AttributeTok{conf\_level    =} \DecValTok{1} \SpecialCharTok{{-}}\NormalTok{ alpha\_adj,}
      \AttributeTok{t\_val         =} \FunctionTok{qt}\NormalTok{(}\DecValTok{1} \SpecialCharTok{{-}}\NormalTok{ (alpha\_adj }\SpecialCharTok{/} \DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{), }\AttributeTok{df =} \FunctionTok{pmax}\NormalTok{(}\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{, N\_Units }\SpecialCharTok{{-}} \DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{)),}
      \AttributeTok{CI\_Lower      =} \FunctionTok{pmax}\NormalTok{(}\DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{, Mean\_Est }\SpecialCharTok{{-}}\NormalTok{ (t\_val }\SpecialCharTok{*}\NormalTok{ SE)),}
      \AttributeTok{CI\_Upper      =} \FunctionTok{pmin}\NormalTok{(upper\_cap, Mean\_Est }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ (t\_val }\SpecialCharTok{*}\NormalTok{ SE))}
\NormalTok{    )}
\NormalTok{\}}

  \CommentTok{\# PAGE 1: STATEWIDE \& TIERED COUNTY EXECUTIVE SUMMARY}

  \CommentTok{\# PAGE 3: INTERACTIVE VIRGINIA \& COUNTY STREET HOTSPOT GIS MAP}
  \FunctionTok{nav\_panel}\NormalTok{(}
    \AttributeTok{title =} \StringTok{"3. Virginia \& Street Hotspot GIS Map"}\NormalTok{,}

        \FunctionTok{actionButton}\NormalTok{(}

          \StringTok{"Zoom Out to Full Virginia Map"}\NormalTok{,}
          \AttributeTok{icon =} \FunctionTok{icon}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"globe{-}americas"}\NormalTok{),}
          \AttributeTok{class =} \StringTok{"btn{-}outline{-}primary w{-}100 mb{-}3"}

        \FunctionTok{radioButtons}\NormalTok{(}

          \StringTok{"2. County Detail Overlay:"}\NormalTok{,}

            \StringTok{"Individual Severe Accident Points"}         \OtherTok{=} \StringTok{"points"}
\NormalTok{          ),}
          \AttributeTok{selected =} \StringTok{"streets"}
\NormalTok{        ),}
        \FunctionTok{radioButtons}\NormalTok{(}
          \StringTok{"p3\_street\_metric"}\NormalTok{,}

            \StringTok{"Severe Accident Rate (\% Severe on Street)"}  \OtherTok{=} \StringTok{"severe\_rate"}\NormalTok{,}
            \StringTok{"Severe Accidents per 100k County Residents"} \OtherTok{=} \StringTok{"severe\_ratio"}
\NormalTok{          ),}
          \AttributeTok{selected =} \StringTok{"severe\_accidents"}
\NormalTok{        ),}
        \FunctionTok{sliderInput}\NormalTok{(}
          \StringTok{"p3\_min\_street\_n"}\NormalTok{,}
          \StringTok{"Min. Total Accidents on Street:"}\NormalTok{,}
          \AttributeTok{min =} \DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{max =} \DecValTok{30}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{value =} \DecValTok{3}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{step =} \DecValTok{1}
\NormalTok{        ),}
        \FunctionTok{sliderInput}\NormalTok{(}
          \StringTok{"p3\_top\_n\_streets"}\NormalTok{,}
          \StringTok{"Number of Top Streets to Display:"}\NormalTok{,}
          \AttributeTok{min =} \DecValTok{5}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{max =} \DecValTok{100}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{value =} \DecValTok{25}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{step =} \DecValTok{5}
\NormalTok{        ),}
        \FunctionTok{hr}\NormalTok{(),}
\NormalTok{        tags}\SpecialCharTok{$}\FunctionTok{small}\NormalTok{(}
          \AttributeTok{class =} \StringTok{"text{-}muted"}\NormalTok{,}
          \FunctionTok{bs\_icon}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"hand{-}index{-}thumb"}\NormalTok{),}
          \StringTok{" Tip: Click any county directly on the Virginia map to zoom in and inspect its highest{-}risk streets, or click \textquotesingle{}Zoom Out to Full Virginia Map\textquotesingle{} above to see the whole Commonwealth."}
\NormalTok{        )}
\NormalTok{      ),}

      \FunctionTok{layout\_column\_wrap}\NormalTok{(}
        \AttributeTok{width =} \DecValTok{1}\SpecialCharTok{/}\DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{,}
        \FunctionTok{card}\NormalTok{(}
          \AttributeTok{full\_screen =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{,}

            \AttributeTok{class =} \StringTok{"bg{-}primary text{-}white"}\NormalTok{,}

\NormalTok{          ),}
          \FunctionTok{card\_body}\NormalTok{(}
            \FunctionTok{leafletOutput}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"p3\_map"}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{height =} \StringTok{"580px"}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{          ),}
          \FunctionTok{card\_footer}\NormalTok{(}
            \AttributeTok{class =} \StringTok{"text{-}muted small"}\NormalTok{,}
            \FunctionTok{textOutput}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"p3\_map\_subtitle"}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{          )}
\NormalTok{        ),}
        \FunctionTok{card}\NormalTok{(}
          \AttributeTok{full\_screen =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{,}
          \FunctionTok{card\_header}\NormalTok{(}
            \AttributeTok{class =} \StringTok{"bg{-}dark text{-}white"}\NormalTok{,}
            \FunctionTok{textOutput}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"p3\_table\_title"}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{          ),}
          \FunctionTok{card\_body}\NormalTok{(}
            \FunctionTok{DTOutput}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"p3\_street\_table"}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{          ),}
          \FunctionTok{card\_footer}\NormalTok{(}
            \AttributeTok{class =} \StringTok{"text{-}muted small"}\NormalTok{,}
            \StringTok{"Includes simultaneous Bonferroni{-}adjusted 95\% binomial confidence intervals for street{-}level severity rates."}
\NormalTok{          )}
\NormalTok{        )}
\NormalTok{      )}
\NormalTok{    )}
\NormalTok{  )}
\NormalTok{)}

\NormalTok{  p1\_selected\_counties }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{reactive}\NormalTok{(\{}

\NormalTok{      input}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{p1\_pop\_tier,}

\NormalTok{    \}}
\NormalTok{    df}

\NormalTok{    df }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{p1\_filtered\_acc}\NormalTok{()}
\NormalTok{    base\_counties }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ county\_pop\_ranks }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}

\NormalTok{    agg }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ df }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
      \FunctionTok{group\_by}\NormalTok{(census\_name) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
      \FunctionTok{summarise}\NormalTok{(}

\NormalTok{      )}

\NormalTok{    base\_counties }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}

      \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}

        \AttributeTok{severe\_ratio     =}\NormalTok{ (severe\_accidents }\SpecialCharTok{/}\NormalTok{ population) }\SpecialCharTok{*} \DecValTok{100000}
\NormalTok{      )}
\NormalTok{  \})}

\NormalTok{  output}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{p1\_kpi\_total }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{renderText}\NormalTok{(\{}
    \FunctionTok{comma}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{sum}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{p1\_county\_summary}\NormalTok{()}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{total\_accidents, }\AttributeTok{na.rm =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{))}
\NormalTok{  \})}

\NormalTok{    tot\_sev }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{sum}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{p1\_county\_summary}\NormalTok{()}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{severe\_accidents, }\AttributeTok{na.rm =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{)}

\NormalTok{    tot\_sev }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{sum}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{p1\_county\_summary}\NormalTok{()}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{severe\_accidents, }\AttributeTok{na.rm =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{)}
    \ControlFlowTok{if}\NormalTok{ (tot\_pop }\SpecialCharTok{==} \DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{) }\FunctionTok{return}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"0.0"}\NormalTok{)}

\NormalTok{    p }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{ggplot}\NormalTok{(}
\NormalTok{      df,}
      \FunctionTok{aes}\NormalTok{(}

      \FunctionTok{geom\_col}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{fill =}\NormalTok{ exec\_navy, }\AttributeTok{alpha =} \FloatTok{0.9}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{width =} \FloatTok{0.68}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{+}

    \ControlFlowTok{if}\NormalTok{ (input}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{p1\_metric }\SpecialCharTok{==} \StringTok{"severe\_rate"}\NormalTok{) \{}

\NormalTok{    \}}

\NormalTok{  p1\_weather\_summary }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{reactive}\NormalTok{(\{}
\NormalTok{    df }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{p1\_filtered\_acc}\NormalTok{() }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
      \FunctionTok{filter}\NormalTok{(}\SpecialCharTok{!}\FunctionTok{is.na}\NormalTok{(Weather\_Condition), Weather\_Condition }\SpecialCharTok{!=} \StringTok{""}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}

\NormalTok{      )}
\NormalTok{  \})}

\NormalTok{  p1\_monthly\_variance }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{reactive}\NormalTok{(\{}
\NormalTok{    df }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{p1\_filtered\_acc}\NormalTok{() }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%} \FunctionTok{filter}\NormalTok{(}\SpecialCharTok{!}\FunctionTok{is.na}\NormalTok{(month\_year))}
